# dbt Fusion changelog
All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

- This file provides a full account of all changes to the fusion lsp
- Changes are listed under the (pre)release in which they first appear. Subsequent releases include changes from previous releases.
- "Breaking changes" listed under a version may require action from end users or external maintainers when upgrading to that version.
- Do not edit this file directly. This file is auto-generated using [changie](https://github.com/miniscruff/changie). For details on how to document a change, see [the contributing guide](CONTRIBUTING.md#adding-a-changelog-entry).

## 2.0.1

Released September 11, 2026

### Breaking Changes

- [dbt-core] Renamed the DuckDB catalogs.yml `duckdb.attach_as` config option to `catalog_database` for consistency with other platforms. ([#15678](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15678))
- [fusion] Rename the lake compute Fivetran auth profile key `fivetran_api_url` to `fivetran_auth_url`

### Features

- [dbt-fusion] (catalogs.yml): add optional catalog-level `description` field to v2 catalogs.yml
- [dbt-core] (catalogs.yml) add optional catalog-level 'owner' and 'meta' fields in v2 catalogs ([#11186](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/11186))
- [dbt-core] Install agent skills (AgentSkills-format `SKILL.md` directories) from installed packages and the project's own `skill-paths` when packages are installed, gated on the new `ai_provider` flag. ([#14556](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14556))
- [dbt-core] Support native Databricks metric view materializations ([#15616](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15616))
- [dbt-extension] VSCE/LSP enable lint fixing via lsp (dbt lint --fix) with dbt.lintFix command and dbt.lintFixOnSave setting
- [fusion] add progress bars to schema cache hydration
- [dbt-extension] LSP exposes source.fixAll.dbtLintFix code action to run lint fix. Update source.fixAll code action to execute combined lint and format in a single command.
- [fusion] Add dbt-platform metadata SDK: authenticated, multi-account access to projects and the Discovery catalog, with automatic OAuth token refresh
- [dbt-index] Add `dbt_rt.relations` to the dbt information schema: one typed row per materialized relation, carrying the warehouse's own `table_type`, `table_owner`, `row_count`, `bytes` and `last_modified`. Also ship `dbt_internal.resources`, a `views.sql` view unioning every resource-type table, for consumers that need one row per resource regardless of type.
- [fusion] Snowflake: parse CREATE SEMANTIC VIEW and SELECT ... FROM SEMANTIC_VIEW(...) (v1, schema synthesized from the query's own METRICS/DIMENSIONS list; no semantic modeling of facts/relationships yet).
- [fusion] Download the version-matched local compute runner on first use.
- [dbt-core] catalogs.yml now defaults to the v2 schema; set `use_catalogs_v2: false` to keep the legacy v1 format ([#15726](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15726))
- [dbt-core] Raise a parse-time error when a model database config collides with a catalogs.yml v2 catalog database without explicit catalog_name. ([#11171](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/11171))
- [dbt-core] Alt auth now has more robust coverage for when to fetch username before running alter code for adding a PAT

### Fixes

- [fusion] Infer unit test expected columns from compiled model SQL
- [fusion] Make --infer-schemas compile downstream nodes through introspective upstream models without connecting to the data warehouse
- [fusion] --infer-schemas: resolve an unqualified column that's ambiguous between multiple open relations if exactly one candidate is confirmed (via other unambiguous references elsewhere in the project) to actually have a column of that name, instead of always leaving it unresolved; warn when it still can't be determined
- [dbt-core] Preserve compiled SQL output for inline compile commands in quiet mode ([#14538](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14538))
- [fusion] Normalize exposure `maturity` values outside `low`/`medium`/`high` to null when resolving exposures, so the emitted manifest stays valid against the published manifest schema
- [dbt-core] [Redshift] Honor the connect_timeout profile field (seconds) so users can bound the wait when an endpoint is unreachable (e.g. IP-restricted cluster). Also accept the dbt 1.x `retries` profile field as an alias for `connect_retries`, and retry connection establishment on transient network / server-side errors (mirrors dbt-redshift Python's default retryable set). ([#16109](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16109))
- [fusion] Fix BigQuery cross-project freshness caching
- [dbt-core] Databricks: `read_files` now typechecks the full set of commonly used options (`sep`, `delimiter`, `quote`, `escape`, `multiLine`, `skipRows`, and more) ([#14434](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14434))
- [dbt-core] Skip materializing BigQuery DML/DDL Arrow results so run_query(INSERT) cannot Storage-Read the destination table and OOM ([#16064](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16064))
- [fusion] Diagnostic logging for state:modified.xxx selections
- [fusion] Error when a state:xxx selector runs without a comparison manifest
- [fusion] Quote string elements when a Tuple (e.g. a run_query column's values()) is stringified for direct SQL embedding, matching Python's tuple repr: a customer macro relying on this idiom to build a comparison/IN-list literal from a query result (a common incremental-watermark pattern) previously produced SQL with the string literal's quotes silently dropped
- [fusion] Translate BigQuery GENERATE_TIMESTAMP_ARRAY, timezone-aware CURRENT_DATE, and SAFE_CAST for local compute.
- [fusion] docs-v2: type/package/tag filters with no search text now return a real filtered list instead of "No results were found"
- [fusion] Fix a panic in column lineage computation when a Window node's schema is stale and shorter than its window_expr count
- [fusion] Fix false-positive state:modified for python models when deferring to a dbt-core 1.12+ manifest, whose checksums are whitespace-normalized
- [dbt-core] Accept a trailing semicolon in a model body: it is now dropped before the body is spliced into a wrapping query, so contract enforcement and `create table as` no longer produce invalid SQL ([#15779](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15779))
- [fusion] Give catalog-fetch worker threads their own root tracing span, so `--write-catalog`/`--write-index` no longer panic and then report a false 60s catalog timeout in debug builds
- [fusion] Revert directory scanning during publication artifact download which results in incorrect project id used in api download request"
- [dbt-index] Refresh the index by ingesting fresh dbt Core artifacts rather than only re-importing existing parquet, based on how the index was last written
- [fusion] Rename dbt v2 CLI branding from Fusion to dbt: the proprietary distribution's --version/--help banner, dbt-repl welcome banner, and version_json output now say "dbt" instead of "dbt-fusion".
- [dbt-index] Fix dbt binary-kind detection and MCP tool descriptions/noise-filtering for the dbt v2 rebrand: the proprietary distribution's --version banner no longer says "Fusion", so detection now defaults to the full distribution when the banner doesn't match a known OSS/Cloud CLI name instead of the reverse.
- [fusion] docs v2: Filter/Assets/Files tab clicks no longer navigate away from the current page or search results; per-type asset lists now show a result count
- [dbt-core] (catalogs.yml): Persist column comments correctly on Snowflake catalog-linked databases (Iceberg REST/Glue/BigLake) ([#15742](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15742))
- [fusion] Declare the @tailwindcss/forms dependency the docs-v2 web app actually requires, instead of relying on it being hoisted from elsewhere in the monorepo
- [dbt-core] (catalogs.yml): duckdb: Attach DuckDB catalogs configured with `endpoint_type: GLUE` or `S3_TABLES` in catalogs.yml to AWS Glue and Amazon S3 Tables. ([#15725](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15725))
- [fusion] Isolate source-freshness failures so one bad source no longer fails every source
- [dbt-core] fix (adapters/bigquery) profile: alias `dataproc_region:` key as `compute_region:`, matching dbt-bigquery's `Credentials._ALIASES`.
- [fusion] docs-v2: sticky asset-detail tab header no longer gets covered by the embedded Lineage panel's toolbar
- [fusion] Preserve Snowflake lateral output qualifiers and SPLIT_TO_TABLE values in local compute
- [fusion] Preserve BigQuery TIMESTAMP instants during DuckDB emission and report unsupported BIGNUMERIC precision before generating invalid SQL.
- [fusion] BigQuery local compute now translates LOGICAL_AND.
- [fusion] Snowflake local compute now translates `PERCENTILE_CONT` to DuckDB continuous quantiles.
- [fusion] Coerce mixed Snowflake VARCHAR and DATE operands in local DATEDIFF
- [fusion] Remove the Snowflake-specific batched `unique` test override so Snowflake uses the default union-all implementation, avoiding the `grouping sets` performance regression. The `unique_field` cast is now adapter-dispatched, so Snowflake uses a plain cast while other adapters keep `safe_cast`'s null-on-failure behavior.
- [fusion] docs-v2: hide non-functional Lenses menu entries (Last run, Last test, Query history) until their data source exists
- [fusion] Improve error messages when requests to the MetricFlow Server fail, surfacing the actual cause (timeout, connection failure, etc.) instead of an opaque retry-exhausted message
- [fusion] Increase connection timeout for sl subcommands
- [fusion] docs-v2: lineage node polish (light-mode edge visibility, expand button label, node text hierarchy)
- [fusion] Avoid DuckDB type-inference errors for heterogeneous Snowflake ARRAY_CONSTRUCT values
- [dbt-core] compute needs wiring for jwt in catalog attach and debug paths ([#14680](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14680))
- [fusion] Stop `dbt lint`/`dbt fmt` from spamming routine per-file completion lines and macro-skip notices to non-interactive output
- [dbt-index] Resolve the dbt project directory independently of index storage, and propagate the selected project, profile, and target through dbt-index execution and MCP artifact refresh.
- [dbt-index] Coordinate dbt-index DuckDB file admission, path handling, and warehouse cancellation.
- [fusion] Prevent metadata replay tests from sharing recording state.
- [internal] Add adapter recordings for the 'dbt debug' command
- [fusion] BigQuery and Snowflake local compute now use native implementations for regular expressions, edit distance, table splitting, and object mutation.
- [fusion] Sidecar: Fixed case insensitivity issues when getting relation info
- [fusion] Honor the selected dbt Platform account when listing projects: an env-var credential for a different account no longer shadows the account-scoped OAuth session, on the initial connection or when the session is later refreshed

### Under the Hood

- [fusion] Sync winget PAT fork with upstream before submitting to avoid opaque wingetcreate submit failures on fork drift
- [fusion] Batch-tests not null query optimized
- [dbt-index] docs v2 now reads the dbt information schema at `target/info_schema/v<n>/` instead of the flat index, and executes the `views.sql` that ships beside it rather than authoring its own `CREATE VIEW` statements. The site's data directory moves with it; `dbt docs generate` builds the information schema itself, so no extra flag is needed.
- [fusion] Update dbt-docs-server README: drop stale private-package/token instructions, add a white-labeling customization guide
- [dbt-core] fix (adapters/databricks) use lowercased catalog, schema, table names in predicates from a SQL query against system.information_schema tables
- [dbt-index] Drop the Fusion brand from docs v2: user-visible copy, comments and crate docs now say "dbt v2". External docs.getdbt.com links keep their existing anchors.
- [fusion] Attribute --infer-schemas columns from declared YAML schema before binding via a CatalogProviderList wrapper, instead of mixing them into InferSchemaRegistry
- [fusion] Gate multi-adapter support (the `adapter` node config and `--adapter`) behind DBT_ENGINE_EXPERIMENTAL_MULTI_ADAPTER
- [fusion] Gate running an individual unit test on local compute (`compute: local` on a unit test) behind DBT_ENGINE_EXPERIMENTAL_LOCAL_UNIT_TESTS
- [dbt-core] Updated README ([#14775](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14775))
- [dbt-index] Fix macOS-only dbt-index unit test failures where temp paths resolve through the /private symlink in DuckDB coordination and dbt runner tests

### Dependencies

- [dbt-core] Bump the DuckDB Extended ADBC driver to `0.21.0.dev+dbt0.0.31` to add native BigQuery and Snowflake parity functions.

## 2.0.0-preview.221

Released September 09, 2026

### Breaking Changes

- [fusion] The lake compute adapter's external name is now lakecompute (no underscore), matching its DbConfig tag: update +adapter:/type: in profiles.yml, dbt_project.yml, and catalogs.yml from lake_compute to lakecompute. lake_compute and the earlier alt spelling are both rejected.

### Features

- [dbt-extension] VSCE/LSP enable formatting via lsp (dbt fmt)
- [fusion] Show a dbt Core style execution index on node result lines, for example `Succeeded model analytics.fct_orders (table) [3 of 53 in 0.34s]`
- [dbt-core] [ClickHouse] Connection-level settings (profile custom_settings plus v1-parity synchronous-operation defaults), default database wired to the profile schema, literal '?' in SQL, and dbt identified as `dbt/<version>` in the HTTP User-Agent (`system.query_log.http_user_agent`), via the new adbc_clickhouse driver ([#14585](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14585))
- [dbt-core] [ClickHouse] dbt unit tests are supported; multi-statement SQL containing ClickHouse backslash escapes is now split correctly by the statement splitter ([#14585](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14585))
- [dbt-extension] Add a plugin-free Neovim integration for the dbt language server, including client-rendered CTE previews.
- [fusion] Deduplicate schema resolution for local unit tests.
- [dbt-core] [ClickHouse] `table` now compatible with MVs (mv_on_schema_change defaults to fail, repopulate_from_mvs_on_full_refresh replays the MV queries), `materialized_view` supports on_schema_change, in-place refresh-schedule updates (MODIFY REFRESH) with safe transition errors, multi-MV update/rename flows and refreshable: false, and projections accept the index form with up-front validation. ([#14585](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14585))
- [fusion] Make dbt-db-runner an opt-in companion for direct Unix installs and preserve it across updates.

### Fixes

- [dbt-fusion] Error at parse time when a model's `+catalog_name` references an undefined catalog or catalogs.yml is missing, instead of silently materializing to the default catalog
- [dbt-core] Preserve complete Databricks streaming-table configuration on rerun and omit empty column comments from create SQL. ([#14612](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14612))
- [dbt-core] Respect full refresh when view_update_via_alter is enabled for Databricks views. ([#14359](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14359))
- [dbt-core] Keep column metadata local to each column instead of inheriting parent resource metadata. ([#16097](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16097))
- [fusion] Expose the runtime config on the `ref` / `source` / `metric` / `function` resolvers, both under `builtins` and at top level, so a custom `generate_schema_name`/`generate_database_name`/`generate_alias_name` can read values such as `builtins.ref.config.project_name` or `source.config.project_name` as it does in dbt Core. For a dependency package's nodes, `.config` now resolves to the ROOT project's runtime config, matching dbt Core, instead of the dependency's own. Also fixes `context.project_name` inside a dependency package's `on-run-start`/`on-run-end` hooks, which resolved to the dependency's own name instead of root's (#13819)
- [fusion] Allow dbt lsp to run in editors without an explicit project directory and ignore unsupported LSP notifications.
- [fusion] Keep dbt lint and dbt fmt progress bars readable by suppressing routine per-file output in interactive mode and routing formatter diagnostics through tracing.
- [dbt-core] Detect Databricks materialized view query changes and require full refresh when compiled SQL changes ([#16123](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16123))
- [dbt-core] Skip Databricks get_relation_config tag and column-tag metadata queries when the model does not configure tags ([#16057](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16057))
- [fusion] A properties file with a top-level `anchors:` key holding a mapping (or any non-sequence YAML node) was rejected with a serialization error, which silently discarded every declaration in that file, including sources referenced by `source()`. `anchors` now accepts any YAML node (#14005)
- [dbt-core] Honor `allow_clones: false` for seeds during run-cache reuse, so a seed is rebuilt instead of cloned across environments like models and snapshots already are. ([#16136](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16136))
- [fusion] Generic data test descriptions authored in schema YAML are now written to the test node's description in the manifest, matching dbt-core
- [dbt-core] [Databricks] Ensure Python models using managed Iceberg get the correct file format
- [fusion] LSP rename model update schema.yml model name
- [fusion] Fix a deadlock that stopped batched generic tests from executing under --batch-tests
- [fusion] drop clone target if it's not a table before attempting clone
- [dbt-core] Preserve `not_null` constraints on nested BigQuery contract fields when generating CREATE TABLE DDL ([#15876](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15876))
- [fusion] Process GetStateSelection requests in batches to handle large node sets
- [fusion] Protect a model's +meta config from the whole-document Jinja render pass and re-render it separately afterward, tolerating a failure instead of propagating it: a render failure nested inside +meta (e.g. an unknown macro call under a misplaced block) no longer drops an entire directory-path node's config -- including unrelated sibling directories' +schema/+materialized/etc overrides -- silently defaulting every model under it
- [fusion] Stop emitting a spurious SnapshotTimestampMismatch (dbt1075) warning for timestamp-strategy snapshots with a timezone-aware updated_at column; the check now only runs when hard_deletes is invalidate or new_record, where snapshot_get_time actually feeds the SCD timestamp columns
- [dbt-core] (dbt-auth): database field is now respected for local and in memory duckdb ([#14196](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14196))
- [fusion] `raw_code` is no longer offered to parse-time checks on the views where it holds nothing: `models`, whose payloads `trim_model_payload` strips of source SQL, and `seeds` and `sources`, which have no SQL to carry. A check filtering on it there reported a confident pass having matched nothing, and the information schema publishes the column populated for models, so a rule developed in `dbt show --info` behaved differently as a check. `snapshots`, `checks`, `analyses`, `hooks` and `functions` keep it -- only model payloads are trimmed.
- [fusion] Cap concurrent microbatch batches per model to stay under Snowflake's DML lock-waiter limit
- [fusion] An empty selection for `dbt check` is now reported once, naming the selection criterion, instead of once per check.
- [dbt-core] Fix unescaped comment clause in materialized view and streaming table macros
- [fusion] Models using a custom materialization now honor their configured `static_analysis` instead of being silently forced to `off`
- [fusion] Preserve Snowflake IGNORE NULLS semantics for LEAD and LAG during local compute
- [fusion] Translate Snowflake ARRAY_CONTAINS, ARRAYS_OVERLAP, extended REGEXP_REPLACE, and TO_CHAR for TIME values in local compute
- [fusion] Preserve BigQuery correlated scalar subqueries and relation-qualified columns across `UNNEST` during local compute
- [fusion] Scope `dbt sl validate` warehouse validation to the selected metrics, semantic models, and saved queries (via MFS's ManifestValidationFilters) instead of always validating the entire manifest, so --select is respected.
- [fusion] Exclude scan-kind edges from dbt docs v2 column-level lineage so a column only reached through a WHERE/JOIN condition no longer appears as a false lineage node
- [fusion] Resolve Azure DevOps private packages when the project segment in packages.yml is percent-encoded, for example `My%20Project`.
- [fusion] Drop a stale VIEW at the dev-clone target before running clone DDL, so cloning a table/incremental relation from prod no longer silently falls back to a full rebuild when dev still has the relation as a view from a prior materialization
- [fusion] Fix stmt_splitter silently dropping malformed statements containing unpaired tokens
- [fusion] `logs/query_log.sql` no longer loses the SQL statement: multi-line adapter errors now carry the `--` comment marker on every line instead of leaking DuckDB's candidate-bindings/`LINE n: ... ^` excerpt as bare SQL, and entries are separated by a blank line so a statement no longer butts against the next entry's `created_at:` header
- [fusion] Fixed a bug where reading columns from a table on lake compute (e.g. via `dbt_utils.star()`, `on_schema_change`, or contracts) could fail with a column-not-found error
- [fusion] `state:modified` now detects a change to a check's `meta`. It was the one config key where a check behaved differently from a model, so editing `meta:` on a check left it unselected while the same edit on a model selected it. `description` is still not compared — consistent with models, where a description-only edit does not select the node either.
- [fusion] Freshness results are published as `dbt_rt.freshness` (was `dbt_rt.source_freshness`) and carry a `resource_type` column of `source` or `model`. `dbt freshness` measures models as well as sources, so the old name described half the contents and there was no way to tell the two apart — the freshness run has always recorded `resource_type`, but the index ingest dropped it.
- [fusion] Handle Databricks shallow clones in clone/table/incremental materializations
- [fusion] Honor the legacy `tests:` alias inside a model's `versions:` block instead of silently dropping it, matching dbt-core, which renames `tests:` to `data_tests:` for versions before parsing them.
- [fusion] Print the Execution Summary even when the catalog fetch times out: abandoned catalog workers no longer hold the invocation span open, which had silently suppressed the end-of-run summary on a `--write-index`/`--write-catalog` build.
- [dbt-core] remove 'connection_name' from the Jinja context used by run operation
- [fusion] Fix dbt tests (generic and singular) not executing against the LakeCompute adapter despite inheriting +adapter: lakecompute; tests were silently falling back to the default adapter
- [fusion] dbt-lsp: fix a self-starvation deadlock where waiting on a just-requested document re-parse (used by completion and signature help) could block the runtime thread the parse itself needed to run, turning a bounded wait into an unconditional full-timeout stall
- [fusion] Information-schema write failures and the parse-tier advisory now carry their own error codes instead of `Generic` (dbt1000): `InfoSchemaWriteFailed` (dbt1657) when `--generate-info-schema` fails, and `InfoSchemaIncomplete` (dbt1658) when the artifact was written from a `parse` and so lacks compiled code, column types, column-level lineage and runtime results. A failed index write on `build`/`run`/`check`, where the index is written by default, reports `IndexWriteFailed` (dbt1659). All three are targetable with `warn_error_options`, which sharing `Generic` made impossible.
- [fusion] Fix the freshness jsonschema's url for built artifact
- [fusion] dbt debug now verifies a Snowflake PAT can be minted even when the project declares no catalogs to attach, so a profile that cannot mint fails at debug time rather than on the first run
- [fusion] `dbt show --info` and info-schema `--inline` read the project metadata directly instead of the published `target/info_schema/` artifact. They no longer need `--generate-info-schema` to have been run, and cannot answer from a snapshot that predates your edits. A project with no metadata yet still reports `InfoSchemaUnavailable` (dbt1656), now naming a command that writes it.
- [fusion] Add static analysis support for BigQuery RANGE
- [fusion] Fix dbt debug and mdls read/write

### Under the Hood

- [dbt-extension] VSCE update default lineage depth to 1
- [dbt-index] Unify the index view surface behind one definition in dbt-index-core: views.sql, the docs/MCP registration, and the DuckDB ingest bootstrap all render from it, with one shared registration path and a DDL portability test across both DuckDB drivers
- [fusion] Expose dbt-dist's --version output classifier so other crates can reuse dbt v1/v2 distribution detection
- [internal] Decode Mantle-replay feature fingerprints into cargo conformance's run-feature analysis, so pass/fail rates can be sliced by which project features a run exercised
- [internal] Introduce dbt-sql-ast: unified SQL abstract syntax tree
- [dbt-index] dbt-index runner forwards rendered dbt log lines to its progress sink so Wizard can stream dbt command output
- [fusion] docs-v2: port the column-lineage mini-graph off dbt-dag onto the new xyflow/dagre engine
- [fusion] Support GCP regression snapshot downloads in f2f repro commands
- [fusion] docs-v2: swap remaining dbt-dag type-only imports (DagNodePanel, useLineageData, dagToolbar) onto local equivalents
- [fusion] Parse-time checks now query the project metadata directly instead of an index built from it, so a failed index write no longer skips the whole check gate.
- [fusion] docs-v2: swap the asset-detail page and full-page lineage route to the OSS LineageV2 engine, delete the old dbt-dag-based views, and remove @dbt-labs/dbt-dag, @dbt-labs/biga, and @dbt-labs/sourdough from package.json

### Dependencies

- [dbt-core] Bump the ClickHouse ADBC driver to 0.1.1: arbitrary connection settings via clickhouse.setting.<name> driver options, default database on connections, and literal ? in SQL no longer treated as a bind parameter. ([#16184](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16184))
- [dbt-core] Bump the dbt Compute ADBC driver to `0.4.0+dbt0.1.7.g9e77239`, adding the `fivetran` auth method (Fivetran PAT exchange).

## 2.0.0-preview.220

Released September 03, 2026

### Features

- [fusion] Add beta support for the Snowflake interactive_table materialization, covering static and dynamic interactive tables and config-change detection for target_lag and warehouse settings
- [fusion] Add support for Python Programmatic Invocations
- [fusion] LSP add parents/children lineage to ref hover
- [dbt-core] Add with behavior flag json format metadata calls. ([#16004](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16004))
- [dbt-core] [ClickHouse] clickhouse_s3source() renders the s3() table function, and model contracts are enforced end-to-end (constraint rendering, per-column codec/ttl, real column types). ([#14585](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14585))
- [dbt-core] [ClickHouse] Incremental strategies now resolve and validate exactly like the Python adapter (delete+insert, insert_overwrite, microbatch), and on_schema_change (fail/append_new_columns/sync_all_columns) works on incremental models with codec-aware ALTERs. ([#14585](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14585))
- [dbt-core] Recognize Databricks persist_constraints and schema.yml meta.constraints so Fusion can persist legacy constraints and unnamed primary-key expressions. ([#15773](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15773))
- [fusion] `dbt system upgrade-distribution` now also recognizes a managed Python project's `dbt-oss` dependency (in addition to `dbt-core`) as upgradeable to the proprietary `dbt` package, and errors clearly if a manifest declares both.
- [dbt-index] dbt-index MCP warehouse queries are now cancelled when their timeout elapses, tearing down the in-flight statement on the warehouse instead of leaving it running
- [fusion] Index and metadata parquet now default to target/private/ instead of target/index and target/metadata. --index-dir, --metadata-dir, DBT_INDEX_DIR, and DBT_METADATA_DIR still override. dbt clean also removes leftover public copies.
- [dbt-core] databricks: support `skip_optimize` model config
- [dbt-index] Add warehouse_query() and change-detection primitives (GenerationToken, generation_token, has_changed_since) to the dbt-index SDK
- [fusion] Add a `propagate` config on models, seeds and snapshots (`+propagate` at project level) naming the compute platforms a node's output is published to after it materializes; accepts a single adapter name or a list, and forces Snowflake binding for a lake compute node whose catalog would not do it implicitly
- [fusion] Add require_hub_verified_downloads: install Package Hub tarballs from the Hub-provided download and verify their sha1 checksums
- [dbt-core] Add config key for snowflake auth to customize application name
- [fusion] Extend `dbt show` to query the information schema via `--info <view>` or `--inline` with `{{ info_schema() }}`.
- [fusion] Add --resource-type/--exclude-resource-type support to dbt freshness
- [dbt-extension] VSCE add command to run dbt-autofix on project
- [fusion] docs v2: replace the generic overview boilerplate with a dashboard landing page (icon cards, copyable commands, live resource counts)
- [fusion] Add 'dbt show --query-id <id>' to fetch a previously completed dbt-compute query's result directly, without re-running the query or hitting a worker; also surface the dbt-compute query id in 'show --inline' output so it can be retrieved later.
- [fusion] Checks now report like tests: one result line per check with its verdict, colour and execution time, and violation rows shown as a table the way `dbt show` shows a query's rows.

### Fixes

- [fusion] persist_docs (Databricks): warn about columns documented in YAML that are not present in the relation
- [fusion] Keep BigQuery replay aligned when MetricFlow execute records precede is_replaceable calls.
- [fusion] Make `--select @model` linear in the size of the graph. Resolving the `@` operator ran one un-deduplicated ancestor walk per leaf node, so its cost grew as (number of leaves) x (paths through the DAG); on a deep DAG with multi-parent models this took minutes. It is now three multi-source traversals. The same per-node walk in `--indirect-selection buildable` is fixed too.
- [dbt-fusion] Disambiguate generated generic-test SQL asset paths on name collisions so tests on different models/columns that flatten to the same generated name are no longer silently dropped from the manifest
- [fusion] Revert schema inference (--infer-schema/--show-sources/--resolve-ambiguous-cols): a correctness gap let a model's inferred schema be reported closed before its upstream schema was actually known
- [fusion] Fix state:modified.body false positive for snapshots with plain outer tags and whitespace-controlled inner Jinja tags
- [dbt-extension] VSCE fix output panel clearing lineage view
- [fusion] Include the `v{version}` segment in the fqn of versioned cross-project (mesh) models so the manifest matches the local and dbt-core v1 parsers. A 2-element fqn previously crashed dbt-core 1.12 node selection with `IndexError` on `dbt build --select <versioned_model>`.
- [dbt-core] Stop the v2 parser emitting a null `macros` key for unit tests that set `overrides` without `macros`, which made dbt-core fail to deserialize the fusion-produced manifest ([#16009](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16009))
- [dbt-core] Match dbt-databricks table-property comparison by retaining all remote properties and detecting changes to configured properties. ([#16051](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16051))
- [dbt-core] BigQuery `api_endpoint` no longer requires trailing slash
- [fusion] `dbt system upgrade-distribution`'s global-install path now probes the package manager to determine whether the existing install is actually `dbt-core` or `dbt-oss` before uninstalling it, instead of always assuming `dbt-core` (which previously left a `dbt-oss` install in place with a silent no-op uninstall).
- [fusion] Fix state:modified missing a snowflake_interactive_warehouses-only config change, which could cause a --select state:modified run to skip a model whose interactive-warehouse attach/detach reconciliation needed to run
- [fusion] Fix blank or whitespace-only refresh_warehouse/snowflake_warehouse values being treated as configured, which could bypass interactive_table's target_lag warehouse validation and render an invalid warehouse = clause in generated DDL for both interactive and dynamic tables
- [fusion] Speed up `--generate-info-schema` by writing `target/info_schema` parquet via DuckDB COPY from epoch views, falling back to Arrow when no driver is available. The flag stays opt-in.
- [fusion] Source, model, snapshot and unit test names written as a YAML literal block scalar (`name: |`) no longer keep their trailing newline, which produced a mismatched unique_id and a spurious DependencyNotFound from `source()` (#13842)
- [fusion] Accept the config value shapes dbt Core tolerates but Fusion rejected - mapping-valued constraints, list-valued dist, and unquoted YAML 1.1 booleans (yes/no/on/off) in contract.enforced, contract.alias_types, docs.show, and the BigQuery partition_by booleans
- [fusion] Populate dbt.hooks from project operations, resolve package-scoped vars with inheritance in dbt.project_vars, fill never-written node columns (access, identifier, language, patch_path, source fields), and list installed packages in dbt.packages.
- [fusion] Keep a singular test that refs a disabled model in the manifest nodes with enabled=false, and stop emitting a user-visible NodeNotFoundOrDisabled warning for it, matching dbt-core
- [dbt-core] [Databricks] Enable changeset computation for liquid clustering ([#15472](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15472))
- [dbt-core] Allow persist_docs configs to be coerced to bools
- [fusion] Replay: canonicalize clock-derived backup relation identifiers (e.g. `_DBT_BACKUP_<timestamp>` from `py_current_timestring()`) so replay no longer reports a false `SqlMismatch (dbt1405)`
- [fusion] Preserve replay compatibility for Python models when internal adapter metadata or blank-only formatting changes.
- [fusion] Time Machine replay: compare python model payloads ignoring blank-line and line-ending layout, so recordings stay valid across changes to how compiled python is assembled
- [dbt-index] dbt_show inline queries no longer inherit the configured favor_state, so ref() resolves to the dev relation when it exists.
- [fusion] Keep a versioned model's latest-version pointer view off the node's own `unique_id`: its distinct replay identity now travels on `TARGET_UNIQUE_ID` alone, so connection pooling, telemetry and cross-version record/replay keep seeing the real node id and existing recordings replay instead of failing with dbt1308
- [fusion] Time Machine replay: answer an adapter read from an already-passed segment when the current one has no match, so recordings whose read ordering shifted with cache state replay instead of failing with dbt1308. Reads are never matched forward across a write barrier the replayer has not reached.
- [fusion] Fix cluster_by change detection for Snowflake dynamic and interactive tables: strip the LINEAR type descriptor SHOW DYNAMIC TABLES wraps cluster_by in, and compare quoted identifiers case-sensitively instead of folding them
- [fusion] Resolve quoting per adapter type when building an adapter, so a non-default adapter no longer inherits the default adapter's quoting policy
- [dbt-core] Support handling for Databricks timestamp_ntz columns in unit tests instead of treating them as non-primitive. ([#16038](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16038))
- [fusion] Support BigQuery timestamp constructors, arithmetic, differences, and TIME values in local compute.
- [fusion] Support BigQuery date-array, FarmHash, JSON, and boolean aggregate translations in local compute.
- [dbt-core] Add missing optimize call to databricks materialization macro
- [fusion] Fix `dbt lint --fix` reporting rule violations (e.g. CV02, AL09, CV10) that live entirely inside a Jinja macro-expanded region -- these are correctly suppressed under plain `dbt lint`, but leaked through under `--fix` with positions pointing into the rendered SQL instead of the source, and could never actually be fixed
- [fusion] Preserve CSV unit-test fixture text until schema-directed casts.
- [fusion] Fix the latest-version pointer view's generated SQL casing to match dbt-core's own (SELECT * FROM, not select * from), so Time Machine's case-sensitive SQL comparison replays existing recordings instead of failing with dbt1308 SqlMismatch
- [dbt-core] [Databricks] Merge `databricks_tags` configs instead of completely overriding them ([#16070](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16070))
- [fusion] Ignore dot-prefixed SQL files during project discovery so Fusion matches dbt Core.

Fixes #14004.
- [fusion] Merge `state:` config keys key by key across config layers, so setting one key on a model, snapshot or test no longer drops the other keys set at the project level (#16135)
- [dbt-core] `dbt lint` no longer fails with dbt1007 when an explicit file target is excluded by `.sqlfluffignore` (or `.dbtignore`); it is now a no-op, consistent with a project-wide lint ([#16089](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16089))
- [dbt-core] [Databricks] Enable column tags for snapshots, tables under materialization v1, and when altering views
- [fusion] Resolve `--adapter` at parse as an override of `+adapter`, so it reaches macro dispatch and routing like any authored selection rather than swapping the run's adapter afterwards
- [fusion] Stop validating the lake compute preconditions at parse, where they rejected nodes an invocation never executes; materialization support and the catalog requirement are reported where the node is written
- [fusion] Render each node against the adapter its `+adapter` selects, so relation rendering and introspective macro calls reach that adapter's connection instead of the target default's
- [fusion] Render a lake compute node's write target, and the propagation relation derived from it, with the node's own adapter rather than the target default; and look up a seed's data-store entry with the same adapter that wrote it
- [fusion] Attach the databases of Snowflake upstreams materialized into Iceberg via `table_format: iceberg`, which have no `catalogs.yml` entry, so lake compute models can read them
- [dbt-core] Quote Snowflake identifiers containing hyphens when rendering SQL so static analysis can resolve them correctly. ([#16103](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16103))
- [dbt-core] BigQuery unit tests no longer leak pseudocolumns ([#15357](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15357))
- [fusion] Preserve Snowflake safe-cast semantics for typed unit-test fixtures.
- [fusion] Preserve window expressions in QUALIFY filters over aggregates for local compute
- [dbt-core] Honor the configured dbt-cloud account_id when authenticating dbt State, instead of using the first session in oauth_sessions.json and reusing a cached token minted for a different account.
- [fusion] Align dbt State's reused/cloned status messages ("Cloned from other environment", "within lag tolerance") with dbt Core's wording, so run_results.json and CLI output stay consistent between engines
- [fusion] Parse-time checks now read the dbt information schema's views and column names. The available views are its `dbt` tables that hold data at parse -- including `unit_tests`, `groups`, `exposures`, `metrics`, `saved_queries`, the semantic-layer tables, `packages` and `project_env_vars`, none of which a check could read before -- plus `graph_nodes` and `checks`. Each exposes the information schema's column names, restricted to those a check can trust at parse. The views still read the index, and the index's own tables now sit in `dbt_internal` rather than `dbt`.
- [dbt-core] Write snapshot run artifacts to `target/run/<pkg>/<original_file_path>/<name>.sql`, matching `target/compiled` and dbt-core. Previously legacy `{% snapshot %}` blocks wrote a flat file at that path, so a `target/` directory left behind by dbt-core v1 (or a second snapshot block in the same file) caused "Is a directory (os error 21)". ([#15692](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15692))
- [fusion] `dbt check` and the `dbt build` check gate now exit non-zero when a check cannot be evaluated. A check whose SQL fails to execute -- a renamed column, a relation that is not there -- was reported as a warning and exited 0, indistinguishable from a check that ran and found no violations, so a check that had quietly stopped working passed CI.
- [fusion] docs v2: keep the asset detail page header and tab nav pinned while scrolling long tab content
- [fusion] docs v2: fix breadcrumb links producing a broken double-hash URL instead of navigating
- [fusion] Widen the UUID-literal canonicalizer used by replay SQL comparison to tolerate whitespace between the quote and the UUID (e.g. an invocation_id-derived audit column literal), so a per-run-unique UUID no longer causes a spurious ReplayDataMissing/SqlMismatch
- [fusion] Widen the ADBC `Database` trait's connection-opening methods to take `&self` instead of `&mut self`, matching the shared, lock-guarded access the underlying implementation already provides.
- [fusion] Parse-time check views are now one-to-one with the dbt information schema: every view a check can read is a table of the same name there, so a query written against `target/info_schema/` runs in a check unchanged. `checks` is now published in the information schema too, `dag_nodes` is available to checks, and the checks-only `graph_nodes` view is gone -- union the per-resource-type views for the same rows.
- [dbt-index] dbt_lint returns a successful not-applicable result instead of an error when the dbt binary cannot run dbt lint, and a failed dbt --version probe is classified as Unknown rather than Core.
- [fusion] Strip double-quote delimiters from a warehouse name before comparing it case-insensitively during dynamic/interactive table configuration-change detection: SHOW never echoes the quotes back, so a quoted warehouse value never compared equal to its own readback and fired a spurious ALTER on every run
- [fusion] Treat a single-quoted string literal inside a clustering expression (e.g. to_char(ts, 'MON')) as case-sensitive data during configuration-change detection: the surrounding expression text was folded correctly, but a literal's exact case, and any )/, characters embedded inside it, previously corrupted the comparison and could cause an unnecessary full refresh on every run. Live-verified against Snowflake
- [fusion] `dbt check <name> --select <subset>` now composes: naming checks chooses which checks run, a selector chooses which of their rows are reported. Naming a check previously switched row scoping off entirely. A zero-row result is also classified more carefully -- a check whose rows were all filtered away because the selection held nothing of the kind it reports on (say `--select tag:quality` where only checks carry the tag) now reports `skipped` rather than a green `pass`.
- [fusion] Translate Snowflake EQUAL_NULL for local compute
- [dbt-core] Reconcile a `target/` directory left behind by a different artifact layout before writing run artifacts. After #14125, snapshots nest under `target/run/<pkg>/<original_file_path>/<name>.sql/<name>.sql`; a pre-#14125 Fusion left a flat file where the nested layout now needs a directory, so upgrading without `dbt clean` crashed replay with "Not a directory (os error 20)". `write_file` now removes a stale file on the parent chain (and a stale directory at a flat-file target) before writing. ([#15692](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15692))
- [fusion] Support set-difference (-) between two Jinja sequences/iterables, matching Python's dict_keys view semantics: a.keys() - b.keys() previously errored (invalid operation: tried to use - operator on unsupported types iterator and iterator) instead of returning elements of a not present in b, breaking every macro relying on this common idiom
- [fusion] Time Machine replay: match `get_columns_in_relation` on relation identity, ignoring the materialization-derived relation-type flags (`is_view`, `is_table`, `is_interactive_table`, ...). A cross-project (Mesh) ref to a public node with no materialization records as `relation_type: None` and replays as `View`, which made valid recordings unreplayable even on the version that produced them.
- [fusion] Default the linter/formatter's jinja render mode to `symbolic` instead of `rendered`, so introspective adapter calls (e.g. `adapter.get_columns_in_relation`) are hole-punched instead of silently rendering as empty/incorrect stub values
- [fusion] Preserve the Snowflake Iceberg table format through `Relation.incorporate()`, fixing incorrect `comment on table` DDL (instead of `alter iceberg table ... set comment`) for Iceberg-backed incremental models
- [fusion] Default the lake_compute adapter's base_url to the production dbt Compute service (overridable via profile or DBT_COMPUTE_BASE_URL) instead of requiring it in profiles.yml
- [fusion] `dbt parse --generate-info-schema` now writes the artifact on a warm parse. On any project that had been parsed before, the partial-load fast path returned the cached compilation before the point where `parse` writes its metadata epochs and information schema, so the command exited 0 having written nothing and printed no warning. Only `parse` was affected: `compile`, `run` and `build` write from a later site the fast path does not bypass.
- [dbt-core] Warn when a dbt project uses the now-deprecated v1 catalogs.yml spec ([#15726](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15726))
- [fusion] `dbt.project_vars` is now one row per (project, variable) on every surface. The information schema no longer duplicates a scoped variable once per package, and checks reading `info_schema('project_vars')` see un-nested variables with a `project_name` column instead of one row per package holding that package's whole vars block.
- [fusion] Return an empty column list when BigQuery get_columns_in_relation targets a missing relation
- [fusion] A secondary (non-default) lake_compute profile output no longer requires database, schema, or method -- database/schema inherit from the default adapter, threads is already shared across adapters, and method defaults to 'token'
- [fusion] A missing or unreadable `target/info_schema/` now reports `InfoSchemaUnavailable` (dbt1656) instead of `InvalidArgument`. `dbt show --info <view>` is a well-formed request against state that has not been generated yet, so reporting it as a bad argument sent people to check their flags.

### Under the Hood

- [dbt-index] dbt-index: unify target/index on the flat parquet layout; legacy epoch-layer directories are collapsed to flat files on open, and both ingestion paths (fusion metadata and dbt-core artifacts) now produce the same file set and column values
- [internal] Sync the canonical Wizard behavioral event schema into proto-rust for Vortex telemetry.
- [dbt-core] Pre-release `dbt-core` sdists (2.x and up) now print an install-time notice pointing at the `dbt` and `dbt-oss` distributions
- [fusion] Remove duplicate AdapterType argument in resolve_xxx functions
- [internal] Extract dbt profile connection schemas into a dependency-light dbt-profile-schemas crate that dbt-init now re-exports (no behavior change).
- [fusion] Rename the lake compute adapter's internal identifiers from `alt` to `lake_compute`, and reject `alt` and `lakecompute` in profiles.yml so `lake_compute` is the only accepted spelling
- [fusion] Carry the run's AdapterStore through to the task runner, so the lake compute adapter is built once from the run's configured factory instead of re-derived per model
- [fusion] Hide --write-index, --write-lineage, and --index-dir from --help. They remain accepted for existing callers. --write-catalog stays visible. --generate-info-schema is unchanged and still opt-in.
- [fusion] Drop dead @dbt-labs/dbt-dag mocks from FilterView tests, left over after the real components stopped using them
- [fusion] Swap docs v2's resource-type vocabulary off @dbt-labs/dbt-dag onto local equivalents
- [fusion] Drop dead @dbt-labs/sourdough imports from FilterView tests and remaining Storybook demos
- [dbt-core] (dbt-auth): Streamling logging in dbt-auth. Streamlines auth config processing overall.
- [dbt-core] BigQuery Foundry experimental driver no longer fails with fake auth error. ([#16081](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16081))
- [fusion] Update windows ASCII art to now show dbt instead of fusion
- [fusion] OSS migration: swap resource-type icons, test-status icons, and remaining dbt-dag exports to local lucide-react equivalents
- [fusion] OSS migration: swap ResourceTypeExplorer/resourceTypesWithColumns off @dbt-labs/dbt-dag onto local equivalents in the lineage panel
- [fusion] OSS migration: swap ResourceTypeExplorer/TransformationType off @dbt-labs/dbt-dag onto local equivalents in the column-lineage view
- [fusion] OSS migration: drop the dead sourdough content-scan from the vendored tailwind preset
- [fusion] OSS migration: drop the redundant sourdough CSS import from app.css
- [fusion] OSS migration: drop the stale sourdough entry from vite's dedupe list
- [fusion] OSS migration: point the vendored tailwind preset at the local biga tokens copy instead of the live package
- [fusion] docs-v2: drop the beta badge and aurora gradient from the topbar, plain background instead
- [fusion] OSS migration: swap AccessLevelIcon/getAccessType off dbt-dag onto local lucide-react equivalents
- [dbt-core] (adapters/bigquery) bump the driver to 0.21.0.dev+dbt0.21.18 ([#15481](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15481))

### Dependencies

- [dbt-core] Bump the Snowflake ADBC driver to `0.21.0.dev+dbt0.21.18`, fixing a rare deadlock in the record reader caused by lazy per-batch channel initialization.

## 2.0.0-preview.219

Released August 28, 2026

### Features

- [dbt-core] Add Databricks query tags with automatic dbt metadata and profile- and model-level configuration. ([#15701](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15701))
- [dbt-core] Fusion accepts Databricks Python environment_key and environment_dependencies and uses them on serverless job submit (https://github.com/databricks/dbt-databricks/issues/1636). ([#15966](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15966))
- [fusion] Add BigQuery `lakehouse_catalog` support to catalogs.yml, enabling 4-part (project.catalog.namespace.table) naming for tables registered in a Lakehouse Runtime Catalog.
- [fusion] Add `--generate-info-schema` to write the dbt information schema: a queryable parquet layer at `target/info_schema/v<n>/` with one table per resource type across the `dbt`, `dbt_rt`, and `dbt_internal` namespaces, plus a generated `views.sql`. The schema version is carried by the directory name, a `dbt.project` column, and a parquet key-value key, so consumers can pin a version. It stages through the flat index at `target/index/`, reusing one when present so the metadata epochs are not ingested twice, and staging privately otherwise so `--no-write-index` never materializes an index. Independent of `--write-index`, which is unchanged.
- [fusion] Add cross project support for model freshness
- [dbt-core] [ClickHouse] Model settings and query_settings configs now render into DDL, and version-gated DDL uses the real server version via a once-per-process probe. ([#14585](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/14585))
- [fusion] Support dbt State-based node selection for state selector in Fusion.
- [fusion] Introduce build_contract_checksum for calculating node contract hash
- [fusion] Rename the dbt-onboard `--fusion-upgrade` flag to `--v2-upgrade` and update onboarding copy to reference dbt v2 instead of Fusion; `--fusion-upgrade` remains as a hidden backward-compatible alias.

### Fixes

- [dbt-core] Databricks incremental models can now set incremental_predicates to a single string instead of a list ([#15771](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15771))
- [dbt-core] `dbt lint` and `dbt format` no longer walk arbitrarily-named hidden or virtualenv directories (e.g. `.venv/`, `.elementary-venv/`) or `tmp/` by default when run without `--select`/`--exclude` ([#15519](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15519))
- [dbt-core] Preserve declaration order when rendering Databricks table properties ([#16036](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16036))
- [fusion] Generic tests now accept a sequence-valued column_name property (e.g. unique: {column_name: [a, b]}), forwarding it as a macro kwarg. A model-level test's column_name kwarg no longer leaks into the test node's own column_name, which names the column the test is attached to and stays null for model-level tests, matching dbt Core (#13607)
- [fusion] Resolve an ephemeral model's own node_id via its seeded alias, not the unique_id's last dotted component, so a versioned ephemeral model (unique_id ending in `.v<N>`) can be matched to its parent's recorded CTE thread
- [dbt-core] Support the microbatch incremental strategy on Databricks ([#15998](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15998))
- [fusion] Databricks: respect `location_root` on models that select no catalog, so external tables are no longer created as managed tables. Also fixes `compute_external_path` to use the relation's `alias` and to omit the database segment unless `include_full_name_in_path` is set.
- [dbt-index] Remove default dbt-index subprocess timeouts and add opt-in timeout_secs controls
- [fusion] Selector method arguments that dbt Core loads as a YAML sequence (e.g. `value: ["models"]`) no longer make Fusion eagerly reject selectors.yml, even when the selector is unused.
- [dbt-core] Databricks: emit INSERT BY NAME REPLACE WHERE only when DBR 18.0+ supports the combination, and warn when insert_overwrite cannot honor REPLACE ON on older clusters ([#15999](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15999))
- [fusion] Fix replay for versioned models with an explicit latest_version: the pointer view's own create-view/grant statements share the base model's recording thread, but dbt-core records them before the base model's own trailing grants, while Fusion creates the pointer view after the base model's materialization (including its trailing grants) completes. The pointer view's calls now carry a distinct replay identity and recover their records via content-based matching instead of the shared sequential cursor
- [fusion] Fix four defects in the dbt index metadata ingest: `dbt.time_spines` was empty for every project and `dbt.models.primary_key` unset, because the model payload was trimmed before the row builders read `__model_attr__`; `dbt.models.config` was `{}` for models with documented columns, because the top-level field lookup searched the payload text and matched a column-level `config` first; `dbt.dag_nodes` kept disabled exposures, metrics and unit tests, which now carry an `enabled` column like other nodes; and the catalog column merge kept only the first column of each node.
- [fusion] Fix a false-positive compile error under --jinja-render-mode symbolic lint: a package macro's own hard error guard (e.g. dbt_utils.date_spine calling exceptions.raise_compiler_error with "upper bound must be positive") could fail the whole render when called with an argument whose real value is unknowable. Such a macro call now short-circuits without running its body, matching how a plain function call already behaves in this case.
- [fusion] Databricks/Spark: `create_schema`/`drop_schema` accept a plain string argument again, matching dbt Core, instead of erroring with `unknown method: string has no method named without_identifier`
- [fusion] Translate BigQuery PARSE_DATE calls for local compute
- [fusion] Canonicalize dialect-specific aliases of config keys
- [fusion] Fix double focus-ring on the search input (missing appearance-none was letting the browser's native <input type=search> decoration leak through)
- [fusion] File tree: any node with children (including a yaml file grouping multiple resources) now renders as a plain folder icon, matching dbt Platform prod -- resource-specific icons only apply at leaf level
- [fusion] Fix three more root causes behind dbt1310 ReplayDataMissing for versioned models with an explicit latest_version: the pointer view inherited contract enforcement from the cloned base model, triggering a get_column_schema_from_query call dbt-core never records for the synthetic pointer; that same call type needed the same full-thread-scan recovery already given to execute(); and the pointer's own CREATE VIEW/grant statements differ from dbt-core's in SQL shape (parens-wrapped body, uppercase SELECT, grants batched into one statement instead of dbt-core's separate ones), now normalized/matched accordingly
- [dbt-core] The download-at-install sdist now declares the runtime dependencies and `requires-python` of the wheel it installs, so `uv pip install dbt-core`/`dbt-oss` no longer produces an unimportable package
- [dbt-core] Databricks: omit the COMMENT clause entirely for columns without a description when building tables with materialization v2, matching dbt-databricks ([#16073](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16073))
- [fusion] Warn when a non-default connection in a profiles.yml target sets `threads:`; thread count is target-wide and comes from the target's default connection (or --threads), so the key is ignored
- [dbt-core] Apply Databricks model and column constraints only when contract.enforced is true ([#16056](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16056))
- [fusion] Fix a CP01 keyword-casing false positive on the synthetic EOF token immediately after a function-paths UDF body's closing parenthesis
- [fusion] Fix ST05 still flagging a correlated JOIN/FROM-clause subquery when the correlation is via a bare (unqualified) column reference, e.g. a lateral `unnest(outer_column)`
- [dbt-core] Fix small divergences between python models whitespace in v1 and v2
- [fusion] Warn when --no-write-index skips parse-time checks, and have dbt retry after a check-blocked build re-run only the failed checks.
- [fusion] Rename check config node_fqn to selection_filter_on so the key names the columns --select scopes on.
- [fusion] BigQuery temporary tables no longer inherit partition_expiration_days from the final table.
- [fusion] Match dbt Core by falling back to the dict constructor for unknown dict method calls in Jinja
- [fusion] Test detail view no longer mislabels generic/singular data tests as unit tests in the breadcrumb
- [fusion] Keep unrecognized Jinja string-literal escapes verbatim instead of unescaping `\/` to `/` (#13816). dbt Core decodes string literals with Python's `unicode_escape` codec, which has no `\/` escape, so a regex pattern such as `"'[+*\/=<> :;.,!?]+'"` built inside a Jinja string now renders identically in Fusion and dbt Core
- [fusion] Replay: preserve the recorded Snowflake table_format when reconstructing relations, so Iceberg models no longer request a drop_relation that dbt-core never recorded
- [fusion] Fixes in state:modified for singular data tests
- [dbt-core] Port Databricks Python scalar-UDF function macros so Python scalar UDFs create and execute on Databricks ([#15777](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15777))
- [fusion] Log error-severity parse-time check failures as errors, not warnings.
- [fusion] Fail parse-time checks via the error counter instead of a second CheckFailed roll-up.

### Under the Hood

- [dbt-core] [databricks] emit lowercased type names
- [fusion] Add an end-to-end replay regression test for versioned models with an explicit latest_version: a single-thread recording (matching how dbt-core records the pointer view's statements on the base model's own thread) now replays successfully
- [fusion] Renamed `dbt system upgrade-distribution`'s help text, prompts, and internal `Distribution` enum (now `Dbt`/`Oss`/`Core`, serializing to `dbt`/`dbt-oss`/`dbt-core`) away from the retired "Fusion" branding, aligning with the dbt v2 naming rollout.
- [fusion] OSS migration: swap sourdough Sizes enum usage to plain string literals in the upgrade-card components and DataPlatformChip
- [fusion] Add unit regression tests for the latest_version_pointer replay fixes: a get_column_schema_from_query full-scan recovery test, a test guarding the full-scan matcher against spuriously matching a non-execute record type, and a test for recovering grants Fusion batches into one statement against dbt-core's separately recorded ones
- [fusion] Replace the regex-based CREATE VIEW parens canonicalization for latest_version_pointer replay with a general, dialect-agnostic fix: extend the existing CREATE ... AS (<subquery>) structural SQL comparison to also recognize a bare (unparenthesized) AS <subquery> body, since the two are always semantically identical. Removes a special-cased regex scoped to one SQL shape in favor of widening an existing, universal equivalence check already used by the shared compare_sql pipeline
- [fusion] Drop the last sourdough Icon usage from the Sources views, matching every other resource list's icon-free Name column
- [fusion] Swap sourdough's Sizes/SizeType imports for local structural types in Button and trust-signal components
- [fusion] Drop sourdough's TanStack pass-through types and a dead Badge-config helper, clearing the last non-lineage sourdough imports from docs v2

## 2.0.0-preview.218

Released August 27, 2026



## 2.0.0-preview.217

Released August 27, 2026

### Features

- [dbt-fusion] Add Exasol adapter (Phase 1): connection/auth with TLS and timeout profile options, metadata adapter, agate/seed type conversion (incl. HASHTYPE) and typed SQL literals, reserved-keyword auto-quoting, and the full macro bundle — materializations (table/view/incremental append·merge·delete+insert·microbatch), snapshots (timestamp/check, hard deletes), model contracts and constraints, on_schema_change, grants, persist_docs for tables and views, catalog generation, source-freshness, cross-database utils (dateadd/datediff/last_day/hash/bool_or/listagg/safe_cast/equals/split_part), distribute_by/partition_by/primary_key configs, and interactive dbt init setup
- [dbt-core] Stub out the lint command in dbt OSS, and warn when strict static analysis is requested without the full dbt distribution installed
- [fusion] Add model freshness schema and validations
- [fusion] Add parse-time project quality checks: SQL files under `checks/` are resolved as nodes, run against the metadata index after parse via `info_schema()` views, and a failing check stops `dbt build` before anything is compiled or executed. `dbt check` runs them on demand.
- [fusion] Add dbt freshness command
- [fusion] Execute Snowflake SQL UDF dependencies in local unit tests
- [dbt-index] dbt-index MCP tool calls now report progress while they work, so the default timeout measures silence instead of total runtime; an agent-supplied timeout_secs remains the opt-in total runtime bound.
- [fusion] Models on the lake compute adapter default to `materialized: table` and `table_format: iceberg`. Both remain plain defaults, so any `+materialized:` or `+table_format:` the author sets -- in dbt_project.yml, schema.yml, or an inline `config()` call -- still wins. The defaults apply to nodes carrying `+adapter: lake_compute` and to every model in a target whose default adapter is `lake_compute`.
- [dbt-core] Show parsed-file counts and in-progress files on the parsing progress spinner ([#15745](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15745))
- [dbt-core] Honor the FORCE_COLOR environment variable so CLI output stays colored when piped through a wrapper process ([#15412](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/15412))
- [dbt-index] Expose dbt compare results through the validation MCP sidecar.

### Fixes

- [dbt-core] Coalesce an explicit empty-string source `schema` / table `identifier` to its default (the source name / table name, respectively). ([#16029](https://github.com/dbt-labs/dbt-core/issues/16029))
- [fusion] Restore end-to-end coverage of column-level classifier propagation: assertions now run against target/index/dbt.node_columns.parquet from inside the test instead of through the checks feature
- [fusion] `fs lint`/`fs format` now parse a `function` node's body from the SQL grammar's `standaloneExpression` entry point (matching the real DDL grammar, `create function ... as <expr>`) instead of treating it as a top-level statement, and identify `function` targets by source path (via the resolved node graph, or the project's `function-paths` config in Turbo mode) instead of a synthetic lint-target id that never matched a real dbt node unique_id -- together fixing CV07 false positives, spurious parse-failure errors, and incorrect DBT05 firing on `function` node bodies shaped like a query (e.g. `(SELECT ...)`, required by the generated `create function ... as (<expr>)` wrapper); RF02 no longer flags BigQuery's implicit `offset` output column from `UNNEST(...) WITH OFFSET`, with or without an explicit `AS alias`, as an unqualified reference
- [fusion] Log dbt State freshness cache misses at DEBUG level instead of WARNING.
- [fusion] Fixed data tests being incorrectly reused after a `--full-refresh` rebuild. dbt State now records full-refresh executions of incremental models and refreshes cached table metadata after any untracked rebuild, so a test is no longer replayed against a table that was just rewritten.
- [fusion] Keep legacy top-level config on version-local column tests warning-only while preserving the configured value.
- [fusion] `fs lint`'s ST05 no longer flags a correlated subquery in a FROM clause (e.g. a lateral `UNNEST` referencing an outer table's column) -- a correlated subquery can't be lifted into a CTE, so the previous unconditional FROM-clause warning was asking for an impossible fix. This extends the existing JOIN-clause correlation exemption (which sqlfluff also has) to FROM, which sqlfluff does not
- [fusion] Load the ADBC driver already present in the cache when a concurrent process installs it first, instead of failing the driver load on Windows

### Under the Hood

- [dbt-core] The oss CLI now builds as a binary named `dbt` instead of `dbt-sa-cli` (build output is at `target/<profile>/dbt`)
- [fusion] Swap the resource-type icon system (models, sources, tests, etc.), file tree, and theme toggle from sourdough Ryecon icons to lucide-react, as part of the docs v2 OSS icon migration.
- [fusion] Swap icon props on the shared Button, Badge, and Input components (and their callers across copy buttons, close/dismiss buttons, lineage panel actions, and status badges) from sourdough Ryecon to lucide-react, as part of the docs v2 OSS icon migration.
- [fusion] Swap the status, health, freshness, and test-result icons (LatestStatusSection, TestResultsSection, TestStatusIcon, UpstreamSourcesSection, TrustSignalDescription, LineageEmptyState, Spinner, constants) from sourdough Ryecon to lucide-react, as part of the docs v2 OSS icon migration.
- [fusion] Vendor sourdough's tailwind typography/color preset into the fs repo for OSS buildability
- [fusion] Databricks: register the SHALLOW CLONE inherited-PK probe as a known Fusion-only deviation, so it no longer causes a false-positive SqlMismatch during conformance replay
- [fusion] Extract in-flight statement tracking into a dedicated dbt-tracked-stmt crate so any ADBC consumer can cancel running queries, and add a token-scoped cancellation sweep alongside the existing cancel-everything sweep
- [fusion] OSS migration: swap remaining sourdough type-only imports (SortingState, DefaultTData) to local/tanstack equivalents
- [dbt-index] Make the dbt-index MCP keepalive test independent of timer resolution

### Security

- [dbt-core] Skip symlink, hard link, and device entries when extracting package tarballs instead of extracting them as-is

## 2.0.0-preview.216

Released August 26, 2026

### Features

- [fusion] Added support for model-wise `table_format: iceberg` config without a catalogs.yml / `catalog_name`, which creates managed Iceberg tables.
- [fusion] Extend `dbt system upgrade-distribution` to support managed Python projects: rewrite a manifest's `dbt-core` dependency to `dbt` (Fusion) at the latest published version, then re-run the project's package manager to bring its lockfile/environment back in sync. Refuses a conda environment's top-level dependency list specifically, since dbt (Fusion) isn't published on conda channels; a `pip:` sub-list entry still upgrades normally. The package manager is now resolved before the manifest is rewritten, so a project whose manager can't be determined is left untouched instead of edited with no way to sync it. Detection no longer depends solely on how the running `dbt` binary itself was installed (often `None` when Fusion ships as a standalone binary) -- it now also probes the project's own directory/PATH for a real package manager, fixing false "couldn't be determined" errors for uv, hatch, rye, and plain pip/asdf/pyenv projects. When detection still can't determine (or rejects) a manager, an interactive prompt now offers to pick one, and a new `--package-manager <name>` flag lets non-interactive/`--yes` runs answer that up front instead of failing
- [dbt-index] dbt build and dbt run now write the parquet index to target/index/ by default, without enabling partial parse; opt out with --no-write-index
- [fusion] Add dbt.hard_coded_reference (DBT05) lint rule: flag qualified relation references built from a hard-coded literal or non-ref/source/this Jinja templating (e.g. var(), target.schema) instead of ref()/source()/this

### Fixes

- [fusion] Re-enable Redshift's `--write-catalog` extended catalog stats (row counts, size, dist/sort keys) via the new generic `AgateTable.join`, and fix four latent bugs in the stats-parsing code this unblocks
- [fusion] Keep the dbt replay stream aligned when a recording holds no record for an adapter call Fusion makes. `rename_relation`, `update_table_description`, `grant_access_to`, `load_dataframe`, `get_dataset_location`, `describe_relation`, `convert_type`, and snapshot target validation now apply their own compatibility behavior instead of consuming an unrelated record, and `get_column_schema_from_query` matches its recorded call by SQL text instead of requiring it at the cursor, mirroring how dbt-core itself replays that call (#13344).
- [fusion] modules.datetime strftime("%f") now renders six zero-padded microsecond digits like Python, instead of nine nanosecond digits

### Under the Hood

- [fusion] Swap the search result icons (project, environment, column count) from sourdough Ryecon to lucide-react, as part of the docs v2 OSS icon migration.
- [fusion] Swap the remaining scattered Ryecon icons (dropdown caret, collapsible-section caret, pill close button, table sort arrows, lineage-view expand toolbar icon, code-snippet/truncated-cell copy icons, upgrade-card dismiss/caret icons, property-card info icon) to lucide-react, as part of the docs v2 OSS icon migration.

### Contributors
- [@aahel](https://github.com/aahel)
- [@ameredith](https://github.com/ameredith)
- [@debrin-og](https://github.com/debrin-og)
- [@lottaquestions](https://github.com/lottaquestions)


## 2.0.0-preview.215

Released August 25, 2026

### Features

- [fusion] Support require_ref_searches_node_package_before_root when resolving unqualified refs, sources, and functions
- [fusion] Support DBT_ENGINE_REQUIRE_REF_SEARCHES_NODE_PACKAGE_BEFORE_ROOT as an environment-variable override for the require_ref_searches_node_package_before_root project flag
- [fs] The lake compute adapter is named lake_compute externally: in profiles.yml `type:`, `+adapter:`, dbt_project.yml `adapters:` keys, catalogs.yml config blocks, the manifest's adapter_type, and Jinja macro dispatch. The former name `alt` is no longer accepted anywhere and is rejected with an error naming the replacement.

### Fixes

- [fusion] A nested dbt project's own config files (dbt_project.yml, packages.yml, dependencies.yml, package-lock.yml, catalogs.yml, vars.yml, profiles.yml, selectors.yml) are no longer validated as resource-properties YAML when an outer project's resource paths walk into that project, so they no longer raise UnusedConfigKey errors.
- [fusion] Create the missing intermediate directory under target/generic_tests/ when an explicit generic test name (e.g. name: customer/id_not_null) contains a path separator
- [fusion] Support trailing comma in jinja multi-item assignment
- [dbt-index] Start the first Wizard chat turn before background dbt project compilation completes, while exposing parse and compile readiness across dbt-index and the relevant app surfaces.
- [fusion] Resolve installed packages by their dbt_project.yml names when package-lock.yml uses a different name.
- [fusion] Mask compact clock bounds in Snowflake temporary-view replay SQL

### Under the Hood

- [fusion] Vendor biga color tokens into the fs repo for OSS buildability
